# V1 delivery program

Status: Proposed

## Purpose

This specification turns the accepted V1 product direction into an ordered,
reviewable, and evidence-gated delivery program from research through shipment.
It inventories the current repository truth, assigns one owner to every
contract, records consolidation decisions, defines work-package interfaces,
maps every product requirement to implementation and evidence, and fixes the
dependency and promotion order.

It is not evidence that V1 works and is not permission to implement the entire
architecture at once. A later work package may begin only when its entrance
criteria are met. A simpler component that passes the same gate remains
preferred over a more complex one. A failed premise, safety boundary, or
resource gate stops or narrows the program instead of being hidden by schedule
pressure.

No software can be proved complete for every future environment. In this
program, **V1 complete** means that the frozen supported claim, configuration,
platforms, languages, and failure boundaries pass every named gate with
inspectable evidence and one reproducible release rehearsal. Everything
outside that envelope remains unsupported.

## Definitions

### Program states

| State | Meaning | Promotion condition |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `Proposed` | Contract or work package is documented but not selected or implemented | Focused review and any required accepted decision |
| `Selected` | Scope, owner, dependencies, interfaces, and gates are frozen for one implementation attempt | Entrance criteria and predecessor receipts pass |
| `Implemented` | Code and tests exist for the selected contract | Repository checks and package verification pass |
| `Evaluated` | Frozen implementation has a retained development or calibration receipt | Named component gate passes |
| `Qualified` | One exact artifact/configuration passes its promotion contract | Independent held-out or sealed gate passes |
| `Shippable` | One release candidate passes G0 through G10 | Signed shipment receipt and rehearsal of the selected closed recovery disposition |
| `Stopped` | A mandatory premise or gate failed | New evidence-backed decision narrows, replaces, or ends the path |

These are delivery states, not specification statuses. A work package never
marks a specification `Validated` merely because code exists.

### Current repository inventory

The inventory at the start of this program is:

| Surface | Current truth | Maturity | Program treatment |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `nemosyne-core` activation kernel | Deterministic ranking of already normalized evidence and inhibition signals with explanations | Experimental implementation | Preserve as a replaceable numerical primitive; do not call it the V1 retriever |
| `nemosyne-evaluation` | Deterministic evaluation of fixed activation parameters | Experimental implementation | Reuse for fixed-intermediate evidence only |
| `nemosyne-evaluation-corpus` | Small versioned coding-agent activation corpus | Experimental evidence | Retain as development regression data; never relabel as sealed V1 evidence |
| Product contract | One local semantically read-only compile call returning attention plus byte-identical prompt; only content-free durable admission coordination may change | Proposed specification; historical Decision 0011 is superseded and its retained boundary is carried forward by Decisions 0014 and 0031 | Canonical observable V1 boundary |
| Numerical cognitive memory and focus | Hybrid exact/numerical memory and focus-candidate contract | Proposed specification; earlier terminal focus decision superseded | Canonical focus branch and memory representation boundary |
| Predictive attention | Transition evidence, alternatives, abstention, observation assessment, and support math | Proposed specification, accepted direction in Decision 0014 | Canonical expectation branch |
| Combined planning | One source-bound focus-and-expectation plan plus one sealed vector-conditioned focus input | Proposed specification, accepted direction in Decisions 0014, 0020, 0034, and 0035 | Canonical adapter-facing semantic and numerical object |
| Focus adapter and renderer | Architecture-neutral vector-conditioned adapter, bounded focus shape, optional lexical realization, exact slots, and independent validation | Proposed specification, accepted direction in Decision 0034 | Must not receive memory text, semantic keys/provenance, validator configuration, become a predictor or answerer, or acquire authority |
| Compile-integrity boundaries | Aggregate-only query flow, exact same shared-set instance, distinct runtime/content/configuration identities, exact-byte collision checks, live conditioning-instance binding, independent validation, closed errors, and no partial success | Proposed specification, accepted direction in Decision 0034, re-adopting superseded Decision 0016's integrity rules | Re-adopts the relevant historical 0015/0016 rules without changing the observable result |
| Memory store, encoder, retriever, expectation kernel, planner, renderer, compiler, and CLI | Not implemented | Proposed only | Build in risk order after predecessor gates |
| V1 usefulness and resource claims | No end-to-end observations | Unproven | G1 headroom before broad implementation; G9 sealed evaluation before claim |

### Consolidation record

Three consolidation passes govern this document set.

| Pass | Conflict or duplication | Resolution |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Semantic | Focus, expectation, goal, action, answer, fact, confidence, and probability were easy to collapse in prose | Canonical distinct types in the predictive-attention specification and requirement `V1-R15` |
| Semantic | “Expectation” could mean one guessed outcome | Finite competing hypotheses grouped only by explicit mutually exclusive alternative family, with unknown and omitted support |
| Semantic | A normalized score could be described as probability | Rename it family-relative evidence share and prohibit probabilistic interpretation until a separate calibrated model passes |
| Semantic | An observed action in a transition could be rendered as advice | Conditions and actions remain distinct; V1 selects no action |
| Architecture | Decision 0011's focus-only product semantics no longer covered qualified prediction | Decision 0011 is superseded by Decision 0014; its local, read-only, one-result, and prompt-integrity boundaries are retained explicitly |
| Architecture | The earlier terminal `AttentionPlan` was focus-only | Decisions 0012 and 0013 are superseded; focus and expectation branch from one shared set and join in `FocusExpectationPlan` |
| Architecture | Focus pruning could erase transition evidence before prediction | Branch from the complete eligible activated set before final plan selection |
| Architecture | Separate renderer calls could diverge | One combined plan, one lexicalization pass, one validator boundary |
| Architecture | Request authority was attached to `Compiler::open` or exposed as a caller-constructible compile input | `Compiler::open` accepts only a bounded untrusted installation locator, resolves bootstrap trust, coordinator capability, and platform handles, then retains only an opaque runtime-registration ticket; each public `compile` accepts only bounded untrusted `CompileCallClaims`, the compiler-owned platform authenticator derives a fresh crate-private `InvocationContext`, and active registries resolve only after per-call admission |
| Architecture | The earlier private-authentication refinement exposed independently mixable projections and overloaded content binding as call identity | One sealed crate-private `AuthenticatedInvocation` owns the complete retained request, authenticated comparisons, trusted authorization time, and fresh call-instance brand; downstream private projectors borrow the aggregate, while one separate nonserializable `InvocationInstanceWitness` preserves call membership without changing semantics |
| Architecture | A free configuration path could bypass installation trust | CLI selects only an authenticated installed configuration identity |
| API | Configuration fingerprint appeared as a second product result | `CompiledPrompt` exposes only compiled bytes; privileged receipts are separate |
| Delivery | Atomic stdout was claimed for an external stream | Buffer before delivery, report exit `10`, and require callers to discard possible partial bytes |
| Mathematical | Compatible outcomes shared one denominator | Normalize only within explicit mutually exclusive alternative families |
| Mathematical | Duplicate and correlated records could multiply support | Content canonicalization plus one bounded support budget per dependency group and family |
| Mathematical | Missing facets and numeric zero were indistinguishable | Retain presence masks and coverage diagnostics even where numerical support is zero |
| Mathematical | Later observation could implicitly rewrite prediction support | Offline assessment uses immutable fixtures; product recomputation requires separately managed evidence and a new explicit compile |
| Mathematical | Planning required an abstention proposition that the expectation set did not supply | Define one exclusive, content-identified, source-bound `FrameAbstentionCandidate` with complete reason/control closure |
| Mathematical | Renderer cost could enter feasibility without a total finite comparison domain or distinguish optional infeasibility from mandatory failure | Define checked nonnegative integer cost domains, empty identities, complete supported-domain bounds, optional infeasibility, mandatory minimum failure, and renderer-owned bound violation |
| Architecture | Bundle serialization order or request-local IDs could silently become semantic planning priority | Keep serialization keys order-only; define static semantic frame, family, and role classes plus separate planning-priority keys |
| Data contract | Transition status and uncertainty had duplicate or undefined ownership | Define complete state/outcome observations, one status field per observation, and distinct observation versus transition uncertainty |
| Mathematical governance | Cross-document notation duplicated formulas and reused symbols | Establish one global notation and derivation-owner registry; focused specifications own formulas and other documents link |
| Mathematical governance | Later refinements left cue absence, graph expansion, executable floating-point normalization, and effective-denominator notation underspecified | Use disjoint present/absent cue states, canonical bounded breadth-by-depth expansion, exact binary-rational accumulation with pinned binary64 division, distinct denominator symbols, and closed typed failures |
| Semantic identity | Frame-local controls and exact values could collide or leak content into model-visible identities | Use one closed frame-qualified expectation-control key domain; let branches carry only a value-, lineage-, and request-independent `ExactSlotOwnerSemanticDescriptor`, let planning alone verify and map that descriptor to `ExactSlotOwnerSemanticKey`, and group exact slots only by the complete `(ExactSlotOwnerSemanticKey, ExactSlotSemanticLocator)` pair; exact content and request-local instances remain privileged sidecars, never semantic or tensor identities |
| Security and validation | Separately supplied query projections, a self-validating signal context, a validator-visible invocation witness, or an injectable validation result could create mixed-call or stale-call joins | Pass one sealed `BoundQuery` aggregate to every downstream boundary, validate the single projected signal context independently against the current sealed invocation, let the compiler alone consume the plan witness and own the private context/product callsite, and expose only a one-way least-privilege read-only view to a no-compiler-dependency validator |
| Compile integrity | Lifetimes, digest equality, reconstructed branch inputs, overloaded configuration access, and request-local validation bindings outside plan identity could not prove one coherent compile | Decision 0034 re-adopts aggregate-only `BoundQuery`, independent invocation/set witnesses, a \(K_R\)-independent `PlanContentId`, exact renderer-configuration identity/content, collision checks, bounded ingress and no partial success from superseded Decision 0016, then adds disjoint configuration views and a private conditioning-instance join |
| Lifecycle integrity | The normative compile sequence skipped admission, durable ticket state contradicted unqualified read-only wording, and update/recovery could reopen access before exact registration and terminal verification | Decision 0031 orders admission before pair-dependent resolution, partitions immutable semantic state from a closed content-free coordination allowlist, and adds update-verification and recovery-registration pending states with fresh generations and deterministic failure handoffs |
| ML research | Parallel learned-predictor slots lacked a typed semantic boundary | Define candidate, abstention, and null slots with frame, horizon, logits, dispersion, and source-attribution constraints without adopting the model |
| Delivery | Implementation order followed component familiarity rather than premise risk | Headroom and evidence harness precede broad architecture implementation |

The consolidation claims above are backed by three named
**non-cryptographic content-bound attestations**. `CONSOL-01..03`,
`REV-01..18`, and `DOC-CONF-24` live at the exact paths declared by the
interim `DOC-00` receipt contract in `docs/receipts/README.md`. Each record
contains its schema version, stable record ID, reviewed relative-path set,
canonical archive algorithm, SHA-256 archive digest, repository commit and
tree, author or reviewer identity, independence declaration where applicable,
UTC completion time, disposition, findings or `none`, and evidence
references. A record is invalid if a required field is absent, its path or ID
differs, the archive cannot be reconstructed, or its digest differs.
Correction before merge replaces the draft at the same exact path; correction
after merge writes a new Git revision at the same canonical path whose
`Replaces` field names the prior record and digest. Git history retains the
earlier revision.

| Receipt / status | Pass and reviewed scope | Current evidence | Reviewer independence and disposition |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `CONSOL-01` / Content-bound attestation | Semantic and mathematical pass over product, memory, predictive, planning, renderer, evaluation, and proof contracts | Exact scope, archive digest, `FND-152..384` reconciliation, and disposition in [`docs/receipts/consolidations/consol-01.md`](../receipts/consolidations/consol-01.md) | Completed only by the referenced record; machine-agent review is not external certification |
| `CONSOL-02` / Content-bound attestation | Architecture/API pass over component graph, authority, interfaces, API/CLI, persistence, renderer, and release | Exact scope, archive digest, `FND-152..384` reconciliation, and disposition in [`docs/receipts/consolidations/consol-02.md`](../receipts/consolidations/consol-02.md) | Completed only by the referenced record; machine-agent review is not external certification |
| `CONSOL-03` / Content-bound attestation | Delivery/evidence pass over packages, dependencies, gates, risks, decisions, and shipment | Exact scope, archive digest, `FND-152..384` reconciliation, and disposition in [`docs/receipts/consolidations/consol-03.md`](../receipts/consolidations/consol-03.md) | Completed only by the referenced record; all empirical choices remain Open |

These attestations are repository review evidence, not digital signatures,
durable evidence custody, external certification, or product validation.
Decision 0022 owns this interim contract. Cryptographic signing, compatible
receipt evolution, and durable custody remain `EVD-01` and Decision 0017.

### Research evidence ledger

Research informs hypotheses and constraints; it does not validate Nemosyne's
implementation or prove biological fidelity.

| Claim used by the program | Source and evidence type | Engineering implication | Limitation and non-proof |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Episodic remembering and imagined future construction recruit overlapping memory systems | Addis, Wong, and Schacter 2007, primary fMRI experiment, “Remembering the past and imagining the future” | Test whether retrieved episode structure can support future-state hypotheses | Neural overlap does not specify a software schema, equation, or benefit |
| Mnemonic prediction errors can bias hippocampal state representations | Bein, Duncan, and Davachi 2020, primary behavioral/neuroimaging experiment, “Mnemonic prediction errors bias hippocampal states” | Preserve transition, temporal, and context structure and test error-sensitive alternatives | Laboratory effects do not prove coding-agent utility |
| Successor-like representations can guide prediction of future events | Ekman, Kusch, and de Lange 2023, primary experiment, “Successor-like representation guides the prediction of future events in human visual cortex and hippocampus” | Treat activated memory as one input to expectation formation | Does not justify treating support as probability or truth |
| Prospection and constructive memory are related but fallible | Buckner 2010 and Schacter et al. reviews | Preserve alternatives, uncertainty, and abstention | Reviews motivate questions; they do not select thresholds |
| Predictions can transform later memories | Bein et al. 2023 review, “Predictions transform memories” | Separate prediction output from independently authorized observation and memory management | Does not imply V1 should learn automatically |
| Attention and expectation can have distinct effects | Zuanazzi, A. and Noppeney, U. (2019), primary experiment, plus Rungratsameetaweemana, N. and Serences, J. T. (2019), review | Keep focus and expectation as separate branches and evaluate their interaction factorially | Experimental definitions vary by task and do not establish Nemosyne's ontology |
| Predictive-processing evidence is mixed and theory-sensitive | Walsh et al. review | State falsifiable software hypotheses and negative controls rather than a universal brain claim | No conclusion about the exact V1 mechanism |
| Set models can represent unordered evidence with learned interactions | Lee et al. 2019, Set Transformer | Retain a learned set predictor as a later comparison after the deterministic baseline | Architecture capacity is not evidence of calibration or superiority |
| Neural confidence is often miscalibrated | Guo et al. 2017 | Never expose model scores as probabilities without a frozen calibration protocol | Classification calibration results do not automatically transfer to expectation sets |
| Distance-aware methods can improve out-of-distribution detection | Sun et al. 2022 | Measure novelty and selective abstention separately from support | OOD score quality is dataset- and representation-specific |
| Selective prediction requires explicit coverage-risk tradeoffs | Gangrade et al. 2021 | Gate positive expectations and report abstention by reason | Theory assumptions may not match generative downstream tasks |
| Local small language models and multilingual checkpoints exist | Official Qwen3 release materials | A local lexicalizer is technically testable | Advertised language support and benchmark scores do not qualify the renderer |
| SQLite supports transactions, WAL, backup, and integrity checks | Official SQLite documentation | It is one viable local persistence candidate to evaluate | Base SQLite does not provide encryption at rest; storage selection remains open |
| Isolated vector-focus feasibility retained more relative behavior with model-native token vectors than with generic semantic embeddings or an aggressive compact codec | Internal synthetic feasibility run, outside this repository and outside the formal evidence path | Keep exact applicability controls outside lossy vectors; compare a deterministic typed baseline before any optional learned realization | Internal synthetic direction only: not G1, V1, factual-memory, downstream-utility, or promotion evidence; no metric is adopted as a threshold |

### Canonical architecture wireframe

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    IN["Complete retained request: prompt, 0..3 situations, contextual evidence, language, budget"] --> ORIGIN["Prompt-origin authentication"]
    CALL["Call claims plus compiler-owned trust inputs"] --> ORIGIN
    ORIGIN --> AI["Sealed AuthenticatedInvocation"]
    AI --> ADM["Active-pair-bound compile admission"]
    ADM --> SNAP["Inside admitted scope: resolved controls plus pinned memory, policy, and preflighted artifacts"]
    IN --> IB["Sealed authenticated and configuration-pinned ingress binding"]
    AI --> IB
    SNAP --> IB
    IB --> Q["Sealed BoundQuery Q with private numerical and exact-binding projections"]
    AI --> SCP["Sole private signal-context projector"]
    SNAP --> SCP
    SCP --> SC["One sealed SignalDerivationContext"]
    AI --> SV["Independent current-invocation validator"]
    SNAP --> SV
    SC --> SV
    DB["One local authorized memory universe"] --> SNAP
    SNAP --> ELIG["Authorization, disclosure, validity, revision, and integrity eligibility M_E"]
    ELIG --> USE["Request-usage compatibility M_Q"]
    Q --> USE
    AI --> USE
    Q --> RET["Bounded candidate retrieval"]
    USE --> RET
    RET --> SIG["Typed signal derivation"]
    Q --> SIG
    SV -->|"V_sig only"| SIG
    SIG --> ACT["Deterministic activation and explanations"]
    ACT --> SET["EligibleActivatedMemorySet"]
    AI -->|"private InvocationInstanceWitness"| SET
    IB -->|"independent B_A lineage projection"| SET
    Q --> F["FocusCandidateSet"]
    SET --> F
    Q --> E["ExpectationBundle of per-frame sets"]
    SET --> E
    F --> PLAN["Canonical FocusExpectationPlan plus VectorConditionedFocusSemanticsV1: C_A and C_V_sem"]
    E --> PLAN
    AI --> PS["Private current-call PlanningInvocationScope"]
    PS --> PLAN
    PLAN --> LIVE["Compiler joins request-local C_V_bind and mints ConditioningInstanceWitness"]
    SNAP --> LIVE
    LIVE --> AV["AdapterConditioningViewV1"]
    LIVE --> CV["FocusConditioningValidationViewV1 with opaque conditioning binding"]
    SNAP --> KS["Compiler splits authenticated K_R"]
    KS --> AK["AdapterConfigurationViewV1"]
    KS --> VK["ValidationConfigurationViewV1"]
    AI --> VC["Compiler-owned post-plan validation-context builder"]
    IN --> VC
    Q --> VC
    SNAP --> VC
    CV --> VC
    AV --> R["Local adapter and renderer to lifetime-borrowed, PlanContentId-sealed RenderedAttention"]
    AK --> R
    PLAN --> SUB["PlanContentId-matched exact substitution"]
    R --> SUB
    VC --> VV["Compiler-owned least-privilege read-only validation view"]
    SUB --> V["Independent conditioning-binding-bound nemosyne-validator over PlanContentId-bound SubstitutedAttention"]
    VV --> V
    VK --> V
    V --> VERDICT["Typed verdict consumed only at the compiler callsite"]
    VERDICT --> SER["Exact byte serializer"]
    IN --> SER
    SER --> CLOSE["Close handles and snapshot; terminalize record and consume ticket"]
    ADM --> CLOSE
    CLOSE --> OUT["attention plus byte-identical user prompt"]

    ADMIN["Privileged memory-management adapter"] --> PUB["Validated immutable revision publication"]
    PUB --> DB
    ADMIN --> REC["Backup, restore, migration, export, and deletion"]
```

The compile path has read-only semantic-storage capabilities plus the sole
narrow `IF-COMPILE-ADMISSION` capability for closed content-free coordination
transitions. Provisioning, memory management, artifact installation, training,
calibration, and evaluation are separate offline or privileged paths. `MEM-03`
owns the privileged management-adapter boundary; the compiler dependency graph
cannot construct or receive that adapter.

Renderer and validation lifetimes prevent a candidate or context from
outliving its borrowed plan and prevent unchecked detachment; they do not
prove that two borrows refer to the same object. The deterministic
`PlanContentId` derived from complete canonical plan content is the sole
candidate-to-plan content identity. The independently sealed
`RendererConfigurationId` identifies the exact authenticated \(K_R\), which
contains every applicable renderer implementation, artifact, precision,
platform-dependent execution selection, resource limit, and other field
capable of changing emitted bytes. Decoder, tokenizer, vocabulary, lexical
model, generation, and decoding fields are present only for a candidate that
uses them and otherwise carry closed explicit absent variants. Equal
configuration IDs therefore imply byte-identical output for successful,
uninterrupted execution over equal plan content and equal deterministic
work-unit, byte, and memory ceilings; a target-platform class groups claims
only and is not an execution identity. Separately constructed
canonical-content-identical plans under matching \(K_R\) must independently
yield identical substitution, validation, and product bytes under that
semantic execution domain. Their live candidate wrappers are not
interchangeable across distinct opaque conditioning bindings. The
compiler quarantines equal `PlanContentId` with different retained canonical
bytes before validator entry; the independent validator owns ordinary
candidate/validation-view `PlanIdentityMismatch` and
`RendererConfigurationMismatch`.

Throughout this program, semantic determinism excludes invocation-local
wall-clock observations, cancellation transitions, and output-transport
behavior. Deterministic byte, work-unit, and memory ceilings are part of the
authenticated semantic execution domain. A claim that an interrupted attempt
returns the same typed outcome additionally requires the same deadline
identity, start instant, cancellation-token generation, observed external-event
trace, and transport behavior. Changed external observations may change
success into a typed operational failure without changing renderer semantics;
every such failure still returns no partial product result.

`API-01` authenticated preflight is the sole trust-establishing constructor of
immutable shared-domain `AuthenticatedRendererConfiguration` values. Compiler
context construction and exact substitution borrow the complete value. The
compiler alone derives unconstructible, nonowning
`AdapterConfigurationViewV1` and `ValidationConfigurationViewV1` values for
their respective domains. Both carry the selected `RendererConfigurationId`
and exact full-\(K_R\) commitment, but neither is an independently
authenticated partial configuration. The adapter view excludes verifier
artifacts, thresholds, calibration, validation-corpus identities, and
validator-only limits; neither view exposes the other's fields. Referent
identity is not the contract: separately authenticated canonical-content-
identical full values are equivalent, while unauthenticated partial
reconstructions or same-ID/different-byte values fail closed. No value or view
grants trust-resolution, update, filesystem, network, registry, installation,
or artifact-mutation capability.

### Work-package contract

Every work package is one normalized record joined by `id` across the full work
breakdown, execution metadata, responsibility, verification, dependency, and
gate registries in this specification. Absence is never inferred: an
inapplicable field is written as `none` with a reason. Every package except the
bootstrap `DOC-00` package is `Proposed`; changing one to `Selected` requires
its named entrance receipt, an accountable human assignment, and any required
accepted decision. The source-owned bootstrap selection below governs
`DOC-00`.

The canonical record is:

```text
WorkPackage
├── id, concise title, status, work type, priority, milestone, and target date
├── owner role and accountable human (`unassigned` until selection; no target
│   date before a human and high-confidence estimate are recorded)
├── objective, user value, and architecture value
├── product requirements and canonical specifications
├── accepted decisions or explicitly open decision
├── explicit predecessors, entrance receipts, and unlocked successors
├── exact scope and non-scope
├── versioned interfaces and artifacts consumed and produced
├── likely files or crates
├── documentation, mathematical, API, and migration impact
├── deterministic tests, adversarial cases, benchmarks, and proof obligations
├── security and privacy review
├── failure behavior and rollback or deletion path
├── measurable acceptance criteria and evidence required before merge
├── complexity estimate and estimate confidence
├── parallel lane and authority-surface conflicts
├── normal or stacked pull-request mode and stack parent
└── contributed gates and residual risks
```

A package is the smallest mergeable unit whose behavior, tests,
documentation, and rollback remain coherent. A pull request may implement one
package or a tightly coupled pair only when the dependency table explicitly
permits stacking. It may not combine an upstream experiment with downstream
production adoption before the evidence is reviewed.

`WorkType` is one of `Documentation`, `Decision`, `ResearchSpike`,
`Implementation`, `Evaluation`, `Qualification`, `Integration`,
`Verification`, or `Release`. Complexity is independently one of `S`, `M`, or
`L`; a research spike is not itself a complexity estimate. Every estimate also
states `low`, `medium`, or `high` confidence. A low-confidence estimate is
planning metadata, not permission to expand scope, and must be replaced by a
measured estimate before selection.

#### DOC-00 bootstrap selection

`DOC-00` establishes the lifecycle and attestation schema that later packages
consume, so it cannot depend on an earlier receipt in that same schema. This is
the sole bootstrap exception:

| Field | Selected value |
| --- | --- |
| Package state at source freeze | `ReviewReady`; the separate evidence commit may advance only to `MergeAuthorized` |
| Owner role | Principal architect |
| Accountable human | `EliasPapavlassopoulos` |
| Selection and target | Selected `2026-07-24`; target `2026-07-24` |
| Estimate | `L-high`, based on twelve non-template specifications, Decisions 0011–0031, three consolidation perspectives, eighteen fixed review perspectives, one deterministic governance checker, and the complete repository verification suite |
| Predecessors and entrance | None; `DOC-00` is the dependency-free `W00` package and its accepted predecessor decisions were present on `main` |
| Interfaces | None; the package changes no runtime or cross-package interface |
| Artifact plan | Freeze one specification-and-decision commit, bind three consolidations and eighteen independent reviews to it, then add one separate merge-authorization evidence commit |
| Test plan | Structural and negative-fixture checks before the source freeze; all repository checks, strict archive reconstruction, exact receipt-set validation, and change-aware documentation validation before merge authorization |
| Security, privacy, and migration | Documentation-only; no private user data, secret, runtime authority, capability, network behavior, or migration enters the artifact |
| Pull-request mode | Normal `task/complete-doc-00` pull request; **Rebase and merge** is mandatory and squash is forbidden |
| Rollback | Revert the integrated DOC-00 commit range before selecting a successor package; retain failed review evidence in Git history |

`DOC-CONF-24` with all subordinate passing attestations authorizes the merge but
does not claim that the branch is already merged or promoted. G0 closes only
when `main` contains the direct, ordered, content-equivalent rebased DOC-00
source/evidence pair and the strict main-push CI run passes. The accountable
human's merge action is the G0 promotion decision; this state is derived from
exact ancestry, content bindings, and CI evidence, never from age.

Two packages may share a parallel lane only after all predecessors pass and
only when the parallel-safety registry shows no incompatible edit to the same
authority surface. A stacked pull request is never a substitute for an
unpassed gate.

### Full work breakdown

#### Foundation and premise

#### Renderer feasibility

`REN-01` begins by freezing the common vector-conditioned adapter, focus-shape,
support-trace, realization, and render-domain contracts before any renderer
source is added. Decision 0034 makes that package the owner of a
dependency-light shared `nemosyne-render-domain` surface containing only sealed
adapter input, validator-only support/provenance input, opaque candidate,
configuration, and content-identity records with checked, non-forgeable
constructors. The compile orchestrator alone splits one
`VectorConditionedFocusInputV1` into disjoint
`AdapterConditioningViewV1` and `FocusConditioningValidationViewV1` values.
Adapters cannot import or inspect the validator-only view. Renderer, validator,
and compiler may depend on the shared domain; the validator never depends on
adapter implementation or compiler-private types. Decision 0034 also preserves
the eleven substitution variants and their deterministic precedence while
leaving the empirical adapter family open.

| ID | Concise title, objective, scope, and non-scope | Depends on | Acceptance and exit evidence | Contributes to |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

It uses admitted query/task vectors, actual selected semantic memory vectors, finite weights, masks, language, bounds, and dense adapter handles and emits one `UntrustedBoundedFocusShapeV1` with an opaque equality-only binding derived from the private conditioning-instance witness and the common closed `FocusSupportTraceV1`; no memory text, semantic key, validator configuration, or validator-only provenance is present. Deterministic realization then recomputes `PlanCanonicalEnvelopeV1`, seals `PlanContentId`, private plan-byte comparison commitment, separate `RendererConfigurationId`, and private exact canonical-\(K_R\)-byte commitment in opaque `RenderedAttention<'plan>`. Exact-slot substitution compares configuration ID/content, plan ID/bytes, slots, exact surfaces, and final cost in fixed order. | `VAL-01` | **Implement the independent semantic validator.** Create the separate `nemosyne-validator` crate as sole implementation owner of conditioning-binding validation over shared opaque candidate-domain types. The compiler lends a least-privilege read-only validation view plus `ValidationConfigurationViewV1`; they retain, respectively, an opaque equality-only conditioning binding and exact full-\(K_R\) commitment matching their paired candidate-side values without exposing either private witness or the complete `AuthenticatedRendererConfiguration`. The validation configuration view alone exposes typed verifier artifacts, thresholds, bounded projection consumption, corruption-corpus identity, and resource functions required for deterministic validation. The validator accepts no independently authenticated partial configuration, projection, or narrower reconstruction and has no dependency on the compiler; it receives neither compiler-private `ValidationContext<'plan>`, `AuthenticatedInvocation`, plan witness, raw plan, exact plan-byte capsule, complete \(K_R\), nor any context-construction, trust, update, filesystem, network, registry, installation, or mutation capability. After the compiler-owned equal-plan-ID/different-plan-byte precheck passes, the validator checks the opaque conditioning binding, candidate/validation-view `PlanContentId`, candidate/validation-view/configuration-view `RendererConfigurationId` and exact canonical \(K_R\) content commitments, and every dense handle through the total validator-only map before bounded semantic checks. It owns `RendererValidationError::PlanIdentityMismatch` and `RendererValidationError::RendererConfigurationMismatch`; the latter includes same-ID/different-\(K_R\)-bytes. Each maps to `InternalInvariantViolation` and exit `70`. It never owns post-expansion `RendererCostBoundViolation`. The lifecycle assignments above include these mandatory subcontracts:
`MEM-03` owns `RegisterOperationalRuntimeV1`, the bounded live registration
registry, conditional close/fence/generation-retirement commands, exact
retirement receipts, and the `UpdateCompileProbeDriverV1` callback contract. It
persists `UpdateClosing` with the complete update owner and captured admission
set before drainage, owns `UpdateTerminalVerificationPending` and
`RecoveryRegistrationPending`, persists the closed content-free compile-
admission records separately from semantic product state, and persists every
purge continuation contract. `MEM-04` implements both crash-atomic
pending-state handoffs and consumes the injected probe callback without a
compiler dependency. G5 uses the contract's conformance driver. Normal commit
or rollback retires every prior registration, atomically installs a fresh empty
runtime-registration generation, requires exact attempt-bound registration
plus the full compile and authenticated management-readiness probes, and either
resumes access or deterministically quarantines without opening it. Successful
quarantine restoration independently retires every prior registration,
atomically installs a fresh empty runtime-registration generation, and requires
exact attempt-bound registration before either normal compile or authenticated
management access resumes;
`MEM-05` preserves the original purge owner and implementation/manifests across
every blocked continuation and reopens normal admission only for an
operational no-effect abort. `REL-01` freezes the permit ledger, the
commit/abort/durable-commit-fence terminal protocol, recovery-capability
terminal retirement, and typed local-installation receipt contracts. `RCV-01`
verifies that timeout and conservative exposure leave a permit outstanding,
that a durably acknowledged fence defeats a resumed old executor, every
capability-terminal race, and the local lifecycle and fresh-generation
bindings.
`REL-03` may emit `PublicationRolledBack` only when every exposed installation
has an exact predecessor-restoration receipt; any local quarantine closes only
as `PartialPublicationQuarantined`.

After invocation authentication and before active-pair-dependent configuration
resolution or artifact preflight, `API-01` acquires exactly one opaque
`CompileAdmissionTicketV1` through `IF-COMPILE-ADMISSION`. Configuration,
artifact, and `MEM-02` snapshot handles are available only inside that admitted
scope and must match its active pair, installation manifest, configuration
registry, executing program, runtime registration, writer epoch, and barrier
generation. `API-01` holds the ticket through the last handle, snapshot, and
stage, closes every handle and snapshot first, and terminalizes the durable
content-free admission record on every success, error, panic-containment, and
cancellation path. Abrupt process loss remains conservatively active until
restart terminalization or generation fencing proves that no holder or
snapshot can survive. It cannot construct, clone, serialize, detach, replay, or
inspect management authority from the ticket. Compile preserves semantic
product state byte- and identity-equally; every durable compile-side transition
must belong to the closed operational-coordination allowlist, contain no
content, and remain unavailable to semantic computation. Gate closure either
captures that ticket or wins before issuance; cancellation request alone does
not count as drainage.

`API-01` also owns the ordinary runtime lifecycle at the public boundary.
`Compiler::open` resolves only bootstrap trust and coordinator capabilities,
then performs exact operational registration as its final fallible step; it
retains only the opaque runtime ticket and no pair-dependent handle.
`Compiler::close(self)` conditionally removes that exact registration, and the
CLI invokes it before stdout delivery with close failure taking precedence.
`API-01` is the sole production implementation of
`UpdateCompileProbeDriverV1`; G8 proves that it executes the exact full compiler
pipeline through the attempt-bound terminal-verification scope. `MEM-04`
receives that driver only through `IF-MEMORY-MANAGEMENT`, and `REL-01` plus
`RCV-01` bind and execute the exact packaged producer.

`API-01` post-plan integrity validation never invokes `bindQuery` or a semantic
encoder a second time. It revalidates the retained request, canonical ingress
binding, and original sealed `BoundQuery` content/integrity commitments
directly. The independent plan-envelope recomputation selected by Decision
0016 remains, but both canonical-envelope passes and every integrity check have
frozen input-byte, elapsed-time, cancellation-check, and peak-workspace
ceilings; `PERF-01` measures each pass separately and rejects an overrun.

Decision 0020 freezes the public success value as an owned
`CompiledPrompt`: `as_bytes()` borrows the complete bytes, `into_bytes()`
returns their ownership, `len()` is the exact byte length, and the guaranteed
UTF-8 view is fallible only at construction and therefore infallible on every
public value. The contract does not require `Clone`. Cancellation is
monotonic, idempotent, authority-free, and checked at every frozen stage and
immediately before the success return. Cancellation observed before that
linearization point wins and returns no `CompiledPrompt`; a later signal
cannot revoke an already returned value. `CLI-01` freezes an OS-specific closed
signal map, first-signal behavior, repeated-signal behavior, unsupported-signal
behavior, stable cancellation exit, and final-return race before source is
added; unsupported platforms narrow support rather than guess.

The same decision makes public constructors the sole semantic classifiers for
empty and whitespace-only text. V1 whitespace is exactly U+0009..U+000D,
U+0020, U+0085, U+00A0, U+1680, U+2000..U+200A, U+2028, U+2029, U+202F,
U+205F, and U+3000; U+200B is not whitespace. Empty remains a distinct case.
CLI parsing delegates to these constructors, and boundary fixtures pin every
included range endpoint, adjacent excluded code point, U+200B, and mixed input.

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

| `MEM-06` | **Research persistent memory evolution.** Define correction and consolidation semantics; never learn implicitly from compile or generated output | Shipped or stopped V1 plus focused memory-evolution decision | Correction provenance, conflict, reversibility, consolidation-loss, and authorization receipt | New post-V1 decision |
| `ML-01` | **Build a governed predictor corpus.** Generate a curated expectation-set training corpus from accepted deterministic semantics and governed transition observations; no model adoption | `EXP-03`, `DATA-01` | Data-governance, lineage, license, and split receipt | New post-V1 decision |
| `ML-02` | **Research a learned set predictor.** Produce typed hypothesis, abstention, and null candidates over the same eligible activated set; no probabilities or open-world prose | `ML-01` | Frozen deterministic-baseline, OOD, permutation, and attribution comparison | New post-V1 decision |

`API-01` is the sole production orchestrator for the Decision-0034 boundary.
After `PLAN-02`, it receives one sealed `VectorConditionedFocusInputV1`,
derives `AdapterConditioningViewV1` and
`FocusConditioningValidationViewV1` exactly once, supplies only the former to
the selected adapter, retains only the latter for validation, and rejects any
unauthorized, unselected, incompatible, duplicate, malformed, or cross-bound
member before adapter execution. The adapter returns one bounded
`UntrustedBoundedFocusShapeV1` and `FocusSupportTraceV1`; candidate-specific
realization, exact substitution, independent validation, and compiled-byte
assembly follow. No product path may substitute memory text, nearest-text
lookup, decimal vector serialization, or validator-only provenance for that
flow.

### Work-package interface and execution metadata

#### Applicability/readiness refinement and next implementation package

Decision 0049 refines the existing package chain without adding a top-level
package or changing its dependency counts:

```text
CORE-01 typed controls/outcomes
→ CORE-02 controls embedded in shared records
→ PLAN-01 applicability evaluation before focus candidates
→ PLAN-02 validated TypedFocusStructureV1
→ REN-01 deterministic realization
→ optional evidence-gated adapter work
```

This order is normative. No attention/focus adapter or LLM integration may
start before `PLAN-02` has emitted and validated the typed structure. Terminal
`Abstain` and `Conflict` bypass every adapter and cannot be overridden.

`CORE-01` is the exact next bounded implementation package, but it is not yet
unlocked: the `EVD-02` harness and `BND-01` fixtures are present on current
`main`, while the package's required passing G1 receipt does not exist. The
package remains `Proposed` until that entrance evidence passes and the
ordinary selection record assigns an accountable human; this statement does
not fabricate selection or empirical readiness. Its implementation candidate
has these exact boundaries:

- **Owning crate/module:** `crates/nemosyne-core/src/applicability/{mod.rs,
  model.rs, error.rs}`, publicly re-exported from
  `nemosyne_core::applicability`.
- **Candidate APIs:** checked constructors for `ApplicabilityBoundaryV1`, its
  closed subject/project, status, revision/cycle, validity,
  supersession/replacement, authority, and priority controls;
  `ApplicabilityOutcomeV1::{Applicable, Abstain, Conflict}`;
  `FocusReadinessOutcomeV1::{Ready, Abstain, Conflict}`; and a dependency-light
  structural shell for `TypedFocusStructureV1`.
- **Non-scope:** no store, encoder, retrieval, activation, focus selection,
  prose, adapter, model, LLM call, persistent migration, or new production
  dependency.
- **Package-local fixtures:** positive all-controls-valid canonical `Ready`;
  negative each missing, malformed, out-of-range, or noncanonical control;
  counterexamples for project match with wrong subject, active record replaced
  by a newer valid revision, stale cycle with high vector similarity,
  lower-authority priority win attempts, equal-authority incompatible
  replacements, and attempts to attach a ready payload or prose to terminal
  `Abstain` or `Conflict`.
- **Measurable merge evidence:** every closed control and error variant;
  canonical ordering/hash equality under input permutation; max/max+1 bounds;
  no ready payload or prose constructor on terminal variants; compile-fail
  proof that callers cannot construct invalid states; existing primitive
  compatibility; `cargo test -p nemosyne-core --all-features --locked`,
  workspace Clippy/Rustdoc/tests, and all documentation gates.

`CORE-01` owns vocabulary and invalid-state prevention only. `CORE-02` owns
record composition; `PLAN-01` owns evaluation of the complete boundary;
`PLAN-02` owns the complete typed focus structure. This split prevents the
first package from silently implementing the memory or focus system.

Complexity is relative (`S`, `M`, or `L`) and does not promise
calendar duration. A low-confidence estimate must be replaced before package
selection. `Parallel` names the lane after dependencies pass. `Normal` means a
pull request against `main`; `Stack 1/2` and `Stack 2/2` are the only
preauthorized two-level stacks.

### Work-package responsibility registry

Every package is owned by the named role. Except for the source-owned `DOC-00`
bootstrap selection above, the accountable human and target date are
`unassigned` for every package while this specification remains `Proposed`;
selection is invalid until an individual, high-confidence estimate, and target
date are recorded in the entrance receipt.
Specification aliases in this table are canonical and do not create parallel
contracts:

- `PC`: `v1-product-contract.md`;
- `ARCH`: `v1-reference-architecture.md`;
- `PROOF`: `v1-proof-program.md`;
- `MEM`: `cognitive-memory-activation-and-focus.md`;
- `EXP`: `predictive-attention-and-expectation.md`;
- `PLAN`: `focus-and-expectation-planning.md`;
- `REN`: `vector-to-attention-renderer.md`;
- `QUAL`: `local-renderer-model-qualification.md`;
- `ACT`: `situation-conditioned-activation.md`;
- `AEVAL`: `activation-parameter-evaluation.md`;
- `CORPUS`: `curated-activation-evidence.md`; and
- `DEL`: this specification.

`D14` is retained as an alias for Decision 0014. Decision 0015 is historical
and has no current alias. Accepted Decisions 0014, 0017–0018, 0020–0022,
0024–0027, and 0029–0038 collectively govern the current DOC-00 baseline;
Decisions 0011–0013, 0015, 0016, 0019, 0023, and 0028 are superseded. Each row
names additional decision dependencies where relevant. `New ADR` means that
selection is blocked until the package's significant long-lived choice is
accepted. Every production-source package updates its owning specification and
Rustdoc in the same pull request; every package updates `DEL` status and
receipt links.

### Interface and artifact ownership registry

Every cross-package transfer uses a content-identified artifact and an immutable
receipt. `ART:<interface-id>:<schema-version>:<digest>` identifies bytes or a
typed in-memory schema; `RCPT:<package-id>:<receipt-version>:<digest>` identifies
the producer's result, all consumed artifact identities, disposition, raw
evidence references, and accountable sign-off. A successor may consume an
artifact only through the producer receipt. Compatible additive evolution
increments the minor schema version; removal, reinterpretation, changed
authority, or changed canonicalization increments the major version and
requires a decision plus consumer migration. Rollback restores the complete
previous producer artifact and all dependent manifests; it never translates a
failed artifact in place.

The owner below is the sole semantic owner. A consumer may adapt transport but
may not redefine meaning, validation, authority, canonical order, or error
behavior.

`Producer` names the package that first emits the real runtime artifact, not
necessarily the package that owns its shared type schema. A consumer package
may be implemented earlier only when an already available schema owner and
`IF-BOUNDARY-FIXTURES` provide checked synthetic values; those fixture builders
are test-only, cannot enter a production call, and confer no runtime producer
receipt. Such a consumer may not run an integration path or claim end-to-end
evidence until the real producer receipt exists. This staged rule applies
specifically to the `EXP-02` consumer of the `CORE-02`-owned `BoundQuery` and
`EligibleActivatedMemorySet` schemas before `SIT-01`/`COMP-01` integration,
and to the `SIG-01` consumer of the `CORE-02`-owned signal-value schema before
`API-01` becomes its sole production projector.

| Interface ID | Producer | Authorized consumers | Contract and authority | Acceptance, compatibility, and rollback |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `IF-EVIDENCE-ENVELOPE` | `EVD-01` | Every evaluation, qualification, verification, and release package | Versioned offline envelope implementing `PROOF-EVIDENCE-RECEIPTS-001` with exactly three disjoint results. Guarded rejection records contain only opaque attempt identity, domain-separated attempted-input or consumed-prefix digest, length, completeness, typed stage/field, allowlisted established identities, one closed reason, and custodian-signed `PreAccessGuardWitnessV1::RejectedAttempt`; no raw bytes/prefixes, outcome fields, or fabricated identities. Missing, invalid, wrong-subject, or mismatched guard evidence produces a validator-authenticated `PreAccessCustodyFailureRecord` with the closed failure class/field and no raw witness, custodian signature, no-access claim, admission, or capability. The closed witness subject is either commitment-bound `RejectedAttempt` with explicit absent-or-established sealed-source state, or manifest-bound `ValidatedRun`. The rejection constructor accepts only the first and compares attempt, kind, commitment, and sealed-source presence/value in fixed precedence. The sole checked `ValidForOutcomeAccess::new(validated_manifest, witness)` accepts only the second and compares the same attempt, exact signed run manifest, sealed source, validation window, closed validation/analysis principal sets, capability state, and access/analysis ledger boundaries in the proof-owned fixed field precedence before producing the sealed admission value. Both paths distinguish missing, structurally invalid, structurally valid wrong-subject, and field-mismatch failures without outer reclassification. Only a successful admission permits a `ValidExperimentReceipt` or sealed capability | Golden round trips, unknown-version rejection, raw-secret/raw-witness non-retention, allowlist and cross-schema construction, early rejection without run-manifest fabrication, cross-attempt replay, rejected-kind/commitment/source-state mismatch, explicit absent/present sealed-source state, fabricated identity/outcome/analysis-ingress rejection, signed protocol without outcome capability, `RejectionGuardMismatch`/`GuardWitnessMismatch` closed-field cases, `MissingGuardWitness`, `InvalidGuardWitness`, and `WrongGuardSubject`, fixed cross-class and multi-mismatch precedence, permutation tests, exact three-way result totality, no false custody/no-access claim, no partial receipt/admission/capability, and custodian-ledger witness verification. A valid witness is conditional custody evidence, not universal proof of no external computation |
| `IF-G1-ENVELOPE` | `TGT-00` | `EVD-02`, `TGT-01`, `E2E-00` | Content-identified pre-outcome envelope resolving every field in `PROOF-G1-CONDITIONS-001`, `PROOF-G1-HEADROOM-001`, and `PROOF-EXPECTATION-BRANCH-001`, including seven injective roles, matched placebo, neutral carrier/token equality, frozen multiplicity, exposure, safety upper bounds, and G1/G9 disjointness | Independent signature only after complete validation and execution identity binding; malformed or mutated fields reject before outcome access. Realized insufficiency under a valid envelope follows the proof-owned pre-arithmetic `Inconclusive` contract |
| `IF-G1-RECEIPT` | `EVD-02` | `TGT-01`, `CORE-01`, release audit | Immutable pass, fail, or `Inconclusive` `ValidExperimentReceipt` that reconstructs the exact envelope, execution, roles, memberships, weights, outcomes, exposures, dispositions, and every result required by `PROOF-G1-CONDITIONS-001`, `PROOF-G1-HEADROOM-001`, `PROOF-EXPECTATION-BRANCH-001`, and `PROOF-EVIDENCE-RECEIPTS-001` | Exists only after valid outcome access; no raw attempted content is imported from a rejection record. Proof-owned invalid-cell semantics apply before arithmetic; no G1 identity, case, outcome, parameter, threshold, or estimand enters G9; fail or `Inconclusive` stops or requires a new envelope |
| `IF-BOUNDARY-FIXTURES` | `BND-01` | Every implementation and verification package | Positive and counterexample fixtures for F1 through F17; no technology selection | Complete boundary coverage; consumer failure blocks merge, fixture correction creates a new identity |
| `IF-TARGET-ENVELOPE` | `TGT-01` | Runtime, qualification, performance, packaging, and release packages | Same-or-narrower supported cohort, platforms, hardware, runtime topology, artifacts, and finite limits | Unsupported-case tests and accepted decision; widening returns to `TGT-00` and G1 |
| `IF-SECURITY-ARCH` | `SEC-00` | `CORE-02`, memory, renderer deployment, compiler, diagnostics, packaging, security verification | Principals, capabilities, trust roots, revocation, process topology, secret and diagnostic policy | Independent threat review; incompatible change supersedes the decision and invalidates downstream security receipts |
| `IF-CORE-PRIMITIVES` | `CORE-01` | All semantic and numerical domain packages | Finite values, stable IDs, canonical maps/sets, domain-separated typed content identities, canonical-byte digests, observed-collision errors, and exact-value primitives; reuse current `nemosyne_core::activation::{CandidateId, ChannelId, UnitInterval}` and the evaluation-owned `ScenarioId` rather than redefining their domains | Type-identity, old/new-path downstream compilation, deprecation, canonical-byte identity, equal-ID/unequal-byte collision, validation/equality/hash/order, property, and duplicate-semantic-domain tests; move/replacement requires a decision, compatibility re-export, and major-version plan |
| `IF-DOMAIN-REQUEST` | `CORE-02` | `SIT-01`, memory authorization, `API-01`, `CLI-01` transport, tests | One complete validated `CompileRequest`: prompt reference, ordered zero-to-three situation statements, caller-supplied contextual time, optional declared location, explicit metadata, optional output language, and optional attention-budget ceiling; requested installed configuration and disclosure are separate `CompileCallClaims` fields owned by `IF-COMPILE-API`; no caller-owned request/situation/content/configuration binding, principal, policy, authorization-view identity, trusted time, capability, or trusted context | Intrinsic request-construction, every request-field presence, unknown identity-field rejection, count/order, compiler-compatibility, request-versus-claim separation, boundary, and authority-isolation tests; no ambient information discovery or transport-owned trust |
| `IF-EVALUATION-PAYLOADS` | `EVAL-01` | `EVAL-02`, `EXP-03`, `ACT-00`, renderer qualification, `E2E-01` | Typed payloads and comparisons inside `IF-EVIDENCE-ENVELOPE`; never redefines the envelope | Permutation and reconstruction tests; payload migration preserves original evidence |
| `IF-CORPUS-REGISTRY` | `EVAL-02` | `EXP-01..03`, `REN-02..05`, `ACT-00`, `E2E-00/01` | Content roots, lineage, licenses, semantic-root split, duplicate and leakage audit | Root-disjointness receipt; exposed sealed roots are permanently retired |
| `IF-TRANSITION-DOMAIN` | `EXP-01` | `EXP-02`, `MEM-02..04`, `ENC-01`, `EXP-03` | Dependency-light logical schema only. The sole checked record constructor derives immutable `TransitionRecordVersionId` from the closed authoritative `TransitionRecordVersionEnvelopeV1`, containing the transition semantics and record-bound `ExactSidecarCustodyBindingV1 { custody_domain_id, sidecar_ref: ExactSidecarRefV1 { regime_id, schema, content_id } }`; it excludes the derived record identity, sidecar back-reference, derived artifacts/indexes, rebuild metadata, and request-local state. Schema, regime, and custody IDs derive from complete immutable definitions. Byte identity preserves a typed content ID only under the identical schema and regime; regime rotation, custody rebinding, exact correction, or reliability migration creates a new record-version value. Nested refs target only verified prior published revisions. A separate pure checked `TransitionFacetArtifact` constructor derives a one-way source-version-bound artifact ID without verifying source existence or publishing | Content-derived schema/regime/custody identity and no-rebind; record/ref/custody envelope inclusion/exclusion; prior-reference and typed nested-reference well-foundedness; same-regime/schema/custody unchanged-content preservation; mutation/caller identity/reference-mismatch/collision precedence; pure artifact construction; chain/version order; reliability boundaries; migration and rollback fixtures. Physical existence, atomic record/sidecar/custody-ledger/projection publication, prelookup validation, retention/erasure eligibility, and complete collision quarantine belong only to `IF-MEMORY-MANAGEMENT`/`IF-MEMORY-REVISION`; numeric equality never establishes compatibility and compile never auto-migrates |
| `IF-EXPECTATION-SETS` | `EXP-02` | `EXP-03`, `PLAN-02`, learned comparison | `deriveExpectationBundle(&BoundQuery, &EligibleActivatedMemorySet<'call>, K)` consumes the complete shared-set instance also supplied to `PLAN-01`, privately projects query views only inside the boundary, validates \(B_Q(Q)=\pi_Q(\Lambda_A)\), preserves both private invocation- and set-instance witnesses, and produces finite per-frame hypotheses after typed reliability admission and piecewise \(\alpha\). No independent query projection or copied, filtered, equivalent, or reconstructed set is accepted. Every hypothesis, frame control, and abstention carries separate instance and lineage-independent semantic identities; evidence shares, coverage-qualified split maxima, unknown/omitted support, abstention, controls, and closed errors remain frame-local | Aggregate-only/split-input compile-fail proof; both branches receive one complete object carrying inseparable \(\Lambda_A\) and both witnesses; invalid query-binding mappings; same-call reconstructed-set distinction; valid same-content repetition; semantic/control collisions; paired-valid nonsemantic-instance noninterference; both-witness preservation; exhaustive reliability, zero-frame, limit, empty-domain, split-maxima, permutation, metamorphic tests; never fabricate neutral reliability or expose support as probability/fact |
| `IF-EXPECTATION-REPORT` | `EXP-03` | `PLAN-02`, G3 audit, `E2E-01` | Reconstructible component outcomes, exact reliability lineage/migration disposition, no-qualified-coverage cases, split-maxima cases, adversarial coverage, and one sealed observation-assessment disposition. A positive prior uses a complete canonical one-to-one map keyed by each and only each exact prior `ExpectationHypothesisInstanceId`; semantic keys cannot substitute for fixture identity | Checked constructor rejects unknown, duplicate, missing, or extra instance keys and returns no partial report. Recompute from payload plus manifest; registered/unregistered migration, permutation invariance, and high-coverage/distant plus low-coverage/near counterexamples are mandatory; corrections create a new receipt |
| `IF-FOCUS-CANDIDATES` | `PLAN-01` after mandatory `COMP-01` completion | `PLAN-02` | Complete `FocusCandidateSet` returned by outer `focusCandidates(&BoundQuery, &EligibleActivatedMemorySet<'call>, K)`. The outer boundary consumes the complete paired-plane `IF-SHARED-SET`, checks \(B_Q(Q)=\pi_Q(\Lambda_A)\), preserves both private witnesses, and independently validates every Request/Memory receipt, attribution, record/artifact/revision, custody binding, and lineage join. Numerical meanings may order only the complete admitted comparison work and contribute bounded relevance; authoritative projections alone determine compatibility, equivalence, grouping, and exact agreement. `IF-COMPILE-API` includes the public `Compiler::open`, `compile`, and consuming
`close` lifecycle. Open performs exact operational registration as its last
fallible step and retains no active-pair-dependent handle; close uses the
conditional registration transition, and the CLI closes before stdout.
`IF-COMPILE-ADMISSION` carries the exact runtime generation, binding digest,
and record-state sequence required by conditional close/fence operations;
whole-generation retirement removes all old rows and retains only the bounded
content-free lifecycle handoff metadata.
For every `IF-COMPILE-ADMISSION`, `IF-MEMORY-MANAGEMENT`, `IF-COMPILE-API`,
`IF-RELEASE-CANDIDATE`, and `IF-RCV-RECEIPT` use of collision terminal
removal, Decision 0036 additionally requires the containment commit to create
`NotStarted` at the exact scope-specific canonical start cursor. Evidence must
cover crash and replay before the first step, every legal first transition,
and rejection of reset, fabricated-outcome, or cursor-mismatch states.

For `IF-DOMAIN-REQUEST`, `IF-COMPILE-API`, and `IF-CLI`, the optional output
language has the conditional semantics in Decision 0037. The prompt resolver
runs exactly once over the original prompt. A matching supported tag confirms
one clearly resolved supported identity; a different supported tag is
`RequestIncompatible`; a valid unsupported tag is `UnsupportedLanguage`; and
a supported tag acts as a fallback only for an unsupported, undetermined,
neutral, or mixed prompt. Request construction owns intrinsic tag syntax only;
authenticated compatibility owns canonicalization and support. A present
unsupported tag has source precedence over simultaneous prompt-support
failures. No interface performs a later language decision.
`API-01` also implements the sole production `UpdateCompileProbeDriverV1`.
`IF-MEMORY-MANAGEMENT` supplies only its attempt-bound probe scope to that
driver, while `MEM-04` consumes the injected implementation. G5 uses the
conformance driver; G8 and the exact release package require the production
binding. `IF-DIAGNOSTICS` remains request-local and nonpersistent throughout
all three public operations.

`IF-G1-ENVELOPE`, `IF-G9-PROTOCOL`, and `IF-G9-RUN-MANIFEST` identify only
fully validated and signed artifacts. A valid candidate-independent G9
protocol grants neither outcome access nor a full experiment receipt; only the
post-RCV run manifest may admit access. Rejected `TGT-00`, `E2E-00`, and
`E2E-02` attempts produce only one owner-defined terminal result. A
`PreAccessRejectionReceipt` requires a valid matching `RejectedAttempt`
witness; unavailable or invalid guard evidence instead produces the minimal
`PreAccessCustodyFailureRecord`. Neither contains raw attempted bytes or a raw
prefix, a fabricated artifact identity, an outcome disposition, or an
analysis admission. Only the guarded rejection has conditional custody
evidence; the custody-failure record makes no no-access claim.
`IF-G1-RECEIPT` and `IF-G9-RECEIPT` exist only after their respective
owner-defined valid-access transition.

#### Closed planning-error adaptation

`PLAN-02` owns the closed `PlanningError` domain; `API-01` owns its public
adaptation and `CLI-01` owns only the stable exit transport. The adapter
preserves the typed planning source and maps every current variant exactly
once:

| `PlanningError` reason | Public `CompileError` | CLI exit | Required disposition |
| --- | --- | ---: | --- |
| `SchemaMismatch` | `InternalInvariantViolation` | `70` | Stop; a preflight-validated schema or immutable branch projection no longer matches |
| `LineageMismatch` | `InternalInvariantViolation` | `70` | Stop; the branch content-lineage join failed |
| `PlanCallBindingMismatch` | `InternalInvariantViolation` | `70` | Stop; at least one branch witness does not match the independently anchored current-call planning scope |
| `UnknownSource` | `InternalInvariantViolation` | `70` | Stop; an item references a source absent from its immutable upstream projection |
| `AuthorityEscalation` | `InternalInvariantViolation` | `70` | Stop; planning attempted to raise an authority or surface-authority ceiling |
| `AllowedUseEscalation` | `InternalInvariantViolation` | `70` | Stop; planning attempted to broaden permitted use |
| `SourceProjectionViolation` | `InternalInvariantViolation` | `70` | Stop; after source existence, authority, and allowed-use checks pass, a residual admitted immutable branch-source field differs from independent source rederivation |
| `InvalidRole` | `PlanningFailure` | `6` | Return the typed planning source; no partial plan |
| `MissingQualifier` | `PlanningFailure` | `6` | Return the typed planning source; no partial plan |
| `MissingRelation` | `PlanningFailure` | `6` | Return the typed planning source; no partial plan |
| `InvalidExpectationDisposition` | `PlanningFailure` | `6` | Return the typed planning source; no partial plan |
| `InvalidPlanningPriority` | `ArtifactUnavailable` | `5` | Repair the malformed or incompatible pinned priority artifact before retry |
| `InvalidExactSlot` | `PlanningFailure` | `6` | Reject an otherwise projection-consistent but invalid or ambiguous slot descriptor, locator, registered owner role or shared meaning, or shape without guessing; branch-owner projection mismatch is not this variant |
| `ConflictingExactSlot` | `PlanningFailure` | `6` | Reject incompatible values or metadata under one exact-slot owner-plus-locator; never collapse independent owners or choose a value |
| `InvalidCostContract` | `ArtifactUnavailable` | `5` | Repair the malformed, incompatible, or falsely declared pinned cost artifact before retry |
| `CostOverflow` | `InternalInvariantViolation` | `70` | Stop; checked arithmetic left the domain accepted during artifact preflight; never saturate |
| `PlanningLimitExceeded` | `ResourceFailure` | `8` | Reject before subset enumeration when canonical closure or member ceilings are exceeded |
| `ConflictingControl` | `PlanningFailure` | `6` | Reject mutually inconsistent validated planning controls; no partial plan |
| `NoFeasiblePlan` | `PlanningFailure` | `6` | Return no partial plan when structural constraints admit none |
| `InsufficientAttentionBudget` | `InsufficientAttentionBudget` | `8` | Return no product result; do not emit a budget-driven empty attention block |

Expectation abstention is valid plan data, not a `PlanningError`. `PLAN-01`
cannot produce `PlanCallBindingMismatch`; it only preserves the opaque witness.
Exhaustive variant tests must fail when any of the twenty source variants is
unhandled, maps to more than one public class, loses its typed source, or
changes exit semantics. Requested-language support is resolved and pinned
before planning under Decision 0020; it is not a `PlanningError`. A later
language mismatch is an authenticated artifact/schema invariant failure, not a
second caller-language classification. The audit asserts
`InsufficientAttentionBudget → InsufficientAttentionBudget → 8` explicitly.

Planning validation uses the table order as its exact variant priority from
top to bottom. Within one variant, it reports the smallest canonical failure
evidence key, defined by the variant's versioned domain tag followed by the
canonical semantic ID tuple and then canonical locator bytes. Collection or
sidecar iteration order never selects the failure.

Renderer substitution separately uses the eleven-variant order preserved by
Decision 0034. An identifier absent from the authenticated registry is
`UnknownSlot`; a registry entry with the global `Forbidden` disposition is
`ForbiddenSlot`; a registry-authorized identifier that is absent from or not
permitted by the selected plan is `SlotBindingMismatch`. Within one variant,
the smallest canonical slot key wins. Multi-invalid permutation fixtures must
reconstruct one identical variant and evidence key.
